Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in fossils theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 351 and the invariant I(A,B) = 240 mod 3.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^240(S, F) is trivial. QED.
